  tests in 444.74 seconds; and the 2662/2662 first full gate in 1102.04 seconds
  with zero tracked-file violations. Exact commit/final gate and James verdict
  remain pending.

## DEC-197 - Apply R2q semantics to successful prefixes and physical rename identity

- **Date:** 2026-08-22
- **Status:** superseded by DEC-198 after James rejected `-009`; no runtime authority
- **Decision:** A terminal's classification does not weaken any semantic
  relationship whose required receipts are already authenticated. Apply title
  route and mapping/asset relationships across accepted, rejected, and pending
  packets; validate debugger-cleanup and game-close receipts even if a later
  receipt fails their enclosing phase; and bind action-9 timing, outcome, and
  prefix hash before any pending return. For successful publication, require
  an authenticated canonical stat of the exact temporary parent, bind its
  inode to the source-parent receipt, and require one filesystem device across
  the temporary, source parent, destination parent, destination, destination
  reread, and terminal tree identities.
- **Why:** James produced fully rehashed `-008` terminals that were impossible
  across receipts yet passed because aggregate semantics existed only on the
  accepted path. He also showed successful-prefix cleanup/close receipts could
  target unrelated PIDs, action 9 could fail after a forged validation prefix,
  and the nominal rename fixtures described cross-filesystem success.
- **Boundary:** This closes only the offline evidence contract. It does not
  authorize or perform a game launch, `/proc` census, ptrace, process-memory
  access, signal, input, screenshot, drive mutation, or terrain/collision
  observation.
- **Evidence:** [[EXP-20260820-005 - R2p task lifecycle diagnostic]], James
  rejection of exact `18591f3424f01eaaa69e0b5430889678df1c09d6` / tree
  `ff65061f6cf3e67ea3c774852f39f3c4a9ffe7bf`; immutable
  `quiet-census-design-009` SHA-256 `c8c451fa...047a1`; analyzer/wrapper/test
  hashes `1dc00b7f...e09e` / `cf02db6b...ccb5` / `a9e99944...7bb`; 192/192
  focused tests in 444.74 seconds; and the 2662/2662 first full gate in 1102.04
  seconds with zero tracked-file violations. Exact commit/final gate and James
  verdict remain pending.

## DEC-198 - Use one conditional R2q dependency reducer and anchor publication stat time

- **Date:** 2026-08-23
- **Status:** superseded by DEC-199 after James rejected `-010`; no runtime authority
- **Decision:** Every semantic relationship whose complete authenticated
  dependencies are present must run through one conditional reducer, regardless
  of accepted, rejected, or pending terminal classification. This includes the
  quiet timing/baseline/guard graph; staged process and process-record hashes;
  title/task/log/asset/mapping links; attach/R2o/continued/stopped-capture and
  completion-image links; cleanup/post-detach; restored mapping/invariant;
  timing/validation/publication; and journal-bound action state. Terminal
  publication must satisfy validation completed < publication outer started <=
  source-parent stat started <= stat completed <= temporary fsync <= rename
  started <= rename completed <= source-parent fsync <= destination-parent
  fsync < reread started <= reread completed <= publication outer completed.
  Comparison publication must begin only after the ordered D1 then D2 post-D2
  rereads complete.
- **Why:** James produced fully authenticated `-009` rejected terminals whose
  title-capture PID or gameplay-log inode disagreed with the staged process or
  fresh log, and terminal/comparison packets with ancient `1..2`
  source-parent stat timestamps. Per-state or partially shared validation made
  individually valid receipts compose into an impossible aggregate.
- **Boundary:** This decision changes only the offline evidence validator and
  synthetic fixtures. It performs and authorizes no game launch, `/proc`,
  ptrace, process-memory access, signal, input, screenshot, drive mapping, or
  terrain/collision observation. A future runtime request remains separately
  gated on James acceptance and fresh explicit user authorization.
- **Evidence:** [[EXP-20260820-005 - R2p task lifecycle diagnostic]], James
  rejection of exact `f81502bd2cbe4915be21d313d1ea1c411c4cd11e` / tree
  `e56942bc2f6fc2b5a0235c5bc469eb05c5c5f9bb`; immutable
  `quiet-census-design-010` SHA-256 `d07d3d5e...effd5c`;
  analyzer/wrapper/test hashes `113fe442...6d13d` / `cf02db6b...ccb5` /
  `e4d7d0b4...baad0`; 226/226 focused tests in 727.47 seconds; and the first
  2696/2696 full gate in 1369.49 seconds with zero tracked-file violations.
  James rejection of exact `889f105aadb0eb55dfb4677750b186540235163c` /
  tree `a93c929f275f58e4a8b55a047e057d6135c62e8f` after independently
  passing 226/226 focused tests in 726.14 seconds.

## DEC-199 - Bind the executed process to the exact executable asset identity

- **Date:** 2026-08-23
- **Status:** James-accepted offline `-011` basis; no runtime authority
- **Decision:** The staged process receipt and asset manifest may not remain
  merely individually valid. Require the executed absolute path and SHA-256,
  plus `(exe_device, exe_inode, exe_size, exe_mtime_ns)`, to equal the exact
  `PC/BUGS/bugs.exe` asset entry's path/SHA and
  `(device, inode, byte_count, mtime_ns)`. Enforce the same shared validator in
  direct accepted-control validation and the conditional reducer whenever
  both receipts are present. Nominal fixtures must derive process stat values
  from the exact asset entry. A regression is coherent only if it recomputes
  every dependent process-guard and terminal envelope hash before expecting
  this binding to reject.
- **Why:** James found that immutable `-010`'s own nominal packet described the
  executed file as `401/402/403/404` but the exact same-path and same-SHA asset
  as `412/422/3/432`. Accepted controls and dependency-applicable rejected and
  pending prefixes all accepted the impossible composition. Equal content
  hashes do not prove that the process executed the inventoried file identity.
- **Boundary:** This decision changes only the offline evidence validator and
  synthetic fixtures. It performs and authorizes no game launch, `/proc`,
  ptrace, process-memory access, signal, input, screenshot, drive mapping, or
  terrain/collision observation. A future runtime remains separately gated on
  an exact James acceptance and fresh explicit user authorization.
- **Evidence:** [[EXP-20260820-005 - R2p task lifecycle diagnostic]], James
  rejection of exact `889f105aadb0eb55dfb4677750b186540235163c` / tree
  `a93c929f275f58e4a8b55a047e057d6135c62e8f`; immutable
  `quiet-census-design-011` SHA-256 `6a8fd3df...c1022` (73,008 bytes);
  analyzer/wrapper/test hashes `e8895a9b...1bfd` / `cf02db6b...ccb5` /
  `472cbabd...32f`; 229/229 focused tests in 740.93 seconds; and the first
  2699/2699 full gate in 1397.31 seconds with zero tracked-file violations.
  James rejection of exact `ed2e655` / `aa5009e0` solely for three false
  historical `-010` byte-count claims; exact three-note correction; and James
  **ACCEPT** of documentation-inclusive commit
  `409cbfbe2b56c271909228d55ea340759b5c6435` / tree
  `de4fa877e4aadae12b64e190ba313dca6e9a8d37`, with unchanged implementation
  hashes, Vault 608/0/8, and clean Git.

## DEC-200 - Make a self-bound completion file the final R2q acceptance boundary

- **Date:** 2026-08-25
- **Status:** superseded by DEC-201 after James rejected exact `-017`
- **Decision:** A retained accepted directory is complete only after a final
  `acceptance-completion.json` `RENAME_NOREPLACE`. Before that boundary, write,
  fsync, and reread the durability receipt; reconstruct and semantically
  validate the complete packet; and stably inventory the exact pre-final root
  and every file's device, inode, byte count, and SHA-256. Bind the completion
  file to its prepared device/inode, validate the completion-bearing packet,
  then rename it without replacement. After rename, fsync the accepted root,
  stably reread the completion and complete tree, compare every pre-final
  identity, and enforce the same publication deadline. Every later D2,
  post-D2, or failure consumer must compare its observed tree to these
  committed identities. A byte-valid exact competitor or an owned envelope
  completed after the deadline remains authenticated pending ambiguity, never
  silent success or an unaccounted failure.
- **Why:** James's `-016` counterexample coherently substituted all observed
  post-D2 root/file identities while preserving names and hashes. `-016` also
  ended its five-second claim before receipt publication and final
  reconstruction, and its failure schema could not represent a complete exact
  competitor. A distinct final file makes completeness observable without
  rewriting already committed bytes and supplies one physical identity for
  exact competitor/late-owner classification.
- **Boundary:** This is an evidence-commit rule, not runtime authority. The
  user's retained authorization covers one 521-point passive P0A `/proc`
  collection with no process-memory access. It cannot activate R2q's ptrace,
  debug-register, capture, or eleven post-seize memory-read ranges. No live
  operation or terrain/collision conclusion follows.
- **Evidence:** James rejection of exact `-016`
  `5217ca08aa4ee7d4beefe49f7c040db36333f477` / tree
  `c0420caeaaf3d462a72a553d412946b5b5f3c048`; 99,969-byte artifact
  `432a4ede18051ac11b6d874202a46fccba963fa231f946b615d956f159448933`;
  [[2026-08-22 1553 - Codex - R2q offline quiet-census design]]. Frozen
  `-017` is 102,029 bytes /
  `c6fe10e278f83f43a60d841c3d1be8dcd68aa63ecd0977ae5517fbe707c92fa7`;
  261/261 design and 59/59 runtime tests pass. The full gate passes
  2,790/2,790 in 1,843.51 seconds with zero tracked-file violations; exact
  commit and James verdict remain pending.

## DEC-201 - Separate R2q envelope validity from durability and benchmark complete publication

- **Date:** 2026-08-25
- **Status:** superseded by DEC-202 after James rejected exact `-018`
- **Decision:** Treat complete-envelope readability/semantic validity and owned
  durability as independent facts. A valid complete envelope with no owned
  successful completion rename is `ambiguous-nonowned-valid-envelope`. After
  an owned rename, record final-directory-fsync, completion-reread,
  tree-reconstruction, tree-verification, and final-deadline stages. Failure
  before full verification is
  `ambiguous-owned-durability-uncertain-envelope`; only full verification
  followed by an actual deadline miss is `ambiguous-owned-late-envelope` and
  durability-confirmed. Compare stable identity without atime, retaining
  device/inode/mode/size/mtime/ctime. Freeze the terminal deadline only after
  an anchored same-filesystem benchmark executes complete production control
  and comparison publication paths, including all semantic validation,
  serialization, durability, reread, tree, and final completion work.
- **Why:** James authenticated exact `-017` and showed that its retained
  2.100-second quiet-packet benchmark did not cover complete terminal or
  comparison publication; the five-second deadline was not executable. He
  also injected a valid envelope before attempt construction that became
  unaccountable, and showed that final-directory-fsync failure was mislabeled
  durable/late because readable files were conflated with durability.
- **Boundary:** The 180-second ceiling is a prospective engineering bound for
  offline evidence publication after cleanup, not a scientific timing fact.
  It grants no runtime authority. The retained user authorization remains one
  passive 521-point P0A `/proc` task-lifecycle collection with no process-
  memory access and cannot activate R2q's ptrace/debug-register, screenshot,
  or eleven post-seize process-memory-read ranges per control. No terrain,
  collision, material, visibility, causality, no-effect, or retail behavior
  follows.
- **Evidence:** James rejection of exact `-017`
  `2267cc5101c1f82222d72154f94b9899185b417f` / tree
  `8166cc4a12e6f7fd02aa280350a7525284d27c08`; artifact 102,029 bytes /
  `c6fe10e278f83f43a60d841c3d1be8dcd68aa63ecd0977ae5517fbe707c92fa7`.
  `terminal-publication-benchmark-004/result.json` is 3,233 bytes /
  `4cb6ed3491f98aaaea35249c880dffc231b9af0a89462a4708d0a28ecd0d7b2c`;
  its worst complete control/comparison paths are 23.167699052/79.340400010
  seconds under 180 seconds. Frozen `-018` is 103,156 bytes /
  `435df6253370efcce1308dae2f0e038be0b45665dbd9456415d51bd70130d382`;
  262/262 design and 68/68 runtime tests pass. The full gate passes
  2,800/2,800 in 2,003.33 seconds with zero tracked-file violations; exact
  commit and James verdict remain pending. See
  [[2026-08-22 1553 - Codex - R2q offline quiet-census design]].

## DEC-202 - Bind R2q late outcomes and benchmark execution provenance

- **Date:** 2026-08-25
- **Status:** superseded by DEC-203 after James rejected exact `-019`
- **Decision:** Classify an owned accepted envelope as late only when the
  completion attempt retains the exact publication-deadline error code,
  deadline value, observed monotonic timestamp, and checkpoint label. Require
  runtime equality with the chained `PublicationDeadlineError`, bind the
  deadline to the completion receipt, require `observed > deadline`, and bind
  the observation to rename and outer-failure chronology for control and
  comparison. Remove the false end-to-end 30-second failure-recording claim;
  retain that duration only for one absolute failed/skipped durable journal-
  transition tail and declare the broader failure-accounting path unbounded.
  Time complete publication only from a clean immutable source commit, record
  the exact executed local source ledger before and after trials, and bind the
  result through a separately tracked data anchor whose addition cannot change
  any timed source byte.
- **Why:** James authenticated exact `-018` but reproduced a validator-accepted
  owned-late assertion whose underlying failure was `DesignError`, an accepted
  30,000,000,001 ns publication-failure record despite the supposed 30-second
  whole-path budget, and a benchmark result that could not prove which current
  production validators/runtime functions it timed. Stage flags, an
  overbroad timing label, and independent current-source hashes were not enough
  to authenticate these claims.
- **Boundary:** This is an offline evidence-accounting and benchmark-provenance
  decision. It grants no launch, `/proc`, ptrace, signal, input, screenshot,
  mapping, or process-memory authority. The retained user authorization remains
  only one passive 521-point P0A `/proc` lifecycle collection without process-
  memory access and cannot activate R2q. No terrain, collision, render grammar,
  material, visibility, causality, no-effect, or retail behavior follows.
- **Evidence:** James rejection of exact `-018`
  `7e6a3b0f1a5c801399c19396aecff71646ac5e02` / tree
  `2c99beff21048e5a5f42df4ee619e7bf83ee74a7`; artifact 103,156 bytes /
  `435df6253370efcce1308dae2f0e038be0b45665dbd9456415d51bd70130d382`.
  Immutable benchmark source commit `3fe483339ae5f96d4a06fa46bf8a3c7ffcc6af58`
  / tree `5953ebf23cdab93656667ace9cc2bb7947b01687`; source ledger
  `01abbe48649c32637b3726ba5b83d8e4cd68a04d9a47846d4670b359a3fa341f`.
  Benchmark `-005`: 4,563 bytes / `64537622...28fa`, worst control/comparison
  22.983197250/78.945117065 seconds. Frozen `-019`: 104,745 bytes /
  `f3d985a5...eadf`; 263/263 design and 68/68 runtime tests pass. The first full
  gate passes 2,801/2,801 in 2,205.27 seconds with zero tracked-file
  violations. See
  [[2026-08-22 1553 - Codex - R2q offline quiet-census design]].

## DEC-203 - Cross-bind R2q owned deadline attempts to production failure semantics

- **Date:** 2026-08-25
- **Status:** accepted in frozen `-020`; James accepted exact `4635a53` /
  `fa5d63d`
- **Decision:** Accept an owned `final-deadline` attempt only when the outer
  record names `R2qRuntimeError` and the exact production final-boundary
  message. Require comparison phase `commit-marker`. Require the deadline
  observation at or before control failure-accounting start and at or after
  comparison start, with both observations no later than outer completion.
  Establish that attempt `stage` has exact string type before closed-set
  membership or indexing, so malformed JSON values always fail as
  `DesignError`.
- **Why:** James authenticated all `-019` provenance and prior corrections but
  reproduced validator-accepted final-deadline records retaining unrelated
  `DesignError` and `final-packet-validation` semantics that production cannot
  emit. He also reproduced a raw unhashable-list `TypeError` by replacing
  `stage` with `[]`.
- **Boundary:** This is an offline schema and evidence-accounting correction.
  It grants no launch, `/proc`, ptrace, signal, input, screenshot, mapping, or
  process-memory authority. Retained authority remains only one passive
  521-point P0A `/proc` lifecycle collection without process-memory access and
  cannot activate R2q. No terrain/collision result follows.
- **Evidence:** James rejection of exact `-019`
  `8e1b98958382886f4df9f39dc5046a9259ac0279` / tree

  complete R2q gate 331/331 in 1,799.86 seconds; first full repository gate

- **Decision:** The proposed P0D successor is not executable and its identity

## DEC-208 - Recover P0E residue only through a predecessor-durable additive envelope

- **Date:** 2026-08-26
- **Status:** consumed; exact failed envelope accepted by James, no retry
- **Decision:** Bind the user's fresh recovery authority to one exclusive
  `P0E-R1-AH-001` attempt. Preserve and never rename/classify the original P0E
  pending packet. Authenticate exact process identities before every signal,
  clear only the exact P0E sink if present, prove stable direct/audit clearance,
  and atomically restore `Y:` only after compare-before-replace guards. Publish
  recovery evidence only as unverified. If interrupted success publication is
  reclassified as failure, authenticate its retained predecessor receipt and
  fsync every predecessor parent whose durable bit is false before moving to a
  validated failed terminal.
- **Why:** The normal-close route was exhausted and two exact residual
  identities remain. James's first audit found a corrupt stage snapshot could
  reach action, failed packets could bless forged retained receipts, and
  publication parents were insufficiently bound. His second audit showed that
  an unfynced pending→published source parent could be hidden by a later
  published→failed move. Both are evidence-integrity defects at a destructive
  cleanup boundary.
- **Boundary:** Candidate source hash `a1a854cf...bb024`, wrapper
  `c14ed69a...f951`, freezer `fafc5acc...30b0`, and tests
  `c58068ed...299f` pass 101/101 focused and 809/809 combined tests. James
  independently passed the focused suite and returned
  `ACCEPTABLE-FOR-SOURCE-FREEZE`; the source-phase full repository gate passed
  3,444/3,444 in 2,500.62 seconds with 0 tracked-file violations. The recovery
  marker and all recovery packet roots were absent at source freeze. This
  source-freeze boundary itself was not live authority.
- **Freeze:** Immutable source commit `dc2481be...f7912f9` / tree
  `ba664464...c1fc88` is bound by the dedicated 2,501-byte activation anchor,
  SHA-256 `df1def84...3556`. The final activation/docs commit was `3aa9127d` /
  `1be8a4a`; its second full gate passed 3,444/3,444 in 2,481.05 seconds. James
  accepted one invocation at that exact clean tree.
- **Evidence:** `reimplementation/ablpc/r2p_p0e_recovery.py`, the recovery and
  activation-freezer wrappers, their focused regressions, and
  [[2026-08-26 1900 - Codex - P0E additive recovery source freeze]].
